## 📌 Snapshot

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Scope | **Every currently-open opportunity reachable for a 2027-batch Indian B.Tech (CS) student** |
| Compiled on | **25 Oct 2026**, 9:47 PM IST (all postings live on the day of compilation) |
| LinkedIn openings captured | **1156** unique job IDs (deduped) |
| — of which already in this repo | 126 (flagged ✅ so you do not double-apply) |
| — **net new to this repo** | **1030** |
| Explicit **2027-batch / 2027 campus-hire** openings | **9** |
| Entry-level / fresher / graduate-trainee openings | **117** |
| Other open entry-level FTE (no batch gate) | **870** |
| Internships | **144** |
| Aggregator 2027-batch internships (stipend + PPO verified) | **20** (14 with PPO) |
| Campus mass-recruit drives tracked | **13** — **7 open now**, 1 rolling, 3 closed / not open |
| Open hackathons (student-eligible, tech) | **67** (28 fully online) |
| *Excluded as mid-level (asks 2+ years of experience)* | *54 — listed in the method section, not in the tiers* |
| **Total opportunities in this brief** | **1256** |
| Sources | LinkedIn guest job-search API (no login), LinkedIn public job pages, Unstop public opportunity API, myinternships.in JSON-LD listings, live checks of official TCS / Infosys / Cognizant / Accenture / Wipro career portals |
| Method | 3,384 LinkedIn job IDs swept across **232 keyword × city × filter queries**, 2,040 full job descriptions fetched and parsed, batch and experience eligibility extracted per posting, every portal link re-checked on the day of writing |

> **What “2027 batch” means here, and why this brief is time-critical.** A 2027-batch B.Tech student joined in **August 2023** and graduates in **May/June 2027**. As of 25 Oct 2026 they are in **4th year, 7th semester** — the final year. That means the entire **2026–27 campus recruitment cycle is open to them right now**, and their placement window closes in roughly **seven months**. Unlike a 2028 batch, a 2027 batch does not need to wait: every drive listed in the next section is a live application they can submit today.

---

## 🚨 Section 0 — Campus Mass-Recruit Drives Open Right Now

> **This is the section that matters most for a 2027 batch, and it is not on LinkedIn.** The mass-recruit campus drives are run through company career portals and placement-cell forms, not job boards — so a LinkedIn sweep misses them entirely. Every status below was **checked on 25 Oct 2026**, and each row names the source that was used, so you can tell a confirmed-open drive from an aggregator claim.

| Drive | Status (25 Oct 2026) | Eligible batch | Key dates / notes | Where |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **TCS iON NQT — November 2026 cycle** | 🟢 OPEN | Batch 2027 | Registration closes **12 Nov 2026**. Test **24 Nov 2026**. Fee ₹999 (IT) / ₹599 (Cognitive, Non-Tech). | https://www.tcsion.com/hub/national-qualifier-test/ |
| **TCS iON NQT — rolling general window** | 🟢 OPEN | 2022–2028 pass-outs | Not a single dated drive — a new test window opens roughly every **2–4 weeks** and stays open to students passing out between 2022 and 2028. | https://www.tcsion.com/hub/national-qualifier-test/ |
| **TCS BPS Hiring 2027** | 🟠 REGISTRATION CLOSED | Batch 2027 | Official page states **Registration status: Closed** (last date was 13 Sep 2026). Batch 2 test is **01 Nov 2026** — already-registered candidates only. Watch for the next window. | https://www.tcs.com/careers/india/tcs-bps-fresher-hiring |
| **TCS BSc Ignite / Smart Hiring 2027** | 🟢 OPEN | Batch 2027 | **IT Associate Trainee**, Pan India. One of the few TCS drives that still takes a **2027 batch**. | https://nextstep.tcsapps.com/indiacampus/ |
| **TCS HackQuest Season 11** | 🟢 OPEN | Batch 2027 (B.Tech) | Online ethical-hacking and cybersecurity contest for **2027 batch B.Tech** students. Prize pool **₹5 lakh**. A contest, not a hiring test — but it is a clean 2027-batch signal to put on a resume. | https://www.tcs.com/hackquest |
| **Infosys Systems Engineer (SE) — campus** | 🟢 OPEN | 2027 batch only | Campus registration form is live and returns HTTP 200. SE exams run through colleges (3–5 Oct, 11–13 Oct done); **SP / DSE in-person coding tests on 25 Oct, 1 Nov, 12 Nov and 6 Dec 2026**. Reported package ₹3.6–4.5 LPA for SE. | https://surveys.infosysapps.com/r/a/SEcampusregistration2027batch |
| **Infosys Mega Hiring — Specialist Programmer / DSE Trainee** | 🟡 VERIFY BATCH ON THE FORM | Announced 2027 | Announced **22 Oct 2026**, reported **₹6.25–21 LPA**. Aggregators disagree on whether this cycle covers 2027 (other reports say 2024–26 batch) — open the official form and read the batch line before spending time on it. | https://www.infosys.com/careers/off-campus.html |
| **Cognizant GenC / GenC Next / GenC Pro / Ace Team / NPN AI & Analytics** | 🟢 OPEN — NO DEADLINE | Batch 2027 | 2027-batch listings are live on Superset and carry **no published deadline**. Cognizant has publicly targeted roughly **24,000–25,000 graduates in calendar 2026** (~20% more than 2025). Colleges receive drive links through the placement cell — **ask your placement officer for your college's link**. | https://careers.cognizant.com/ |
| **Salesforce — Futureforce University Program (Software Engineer AMTS)** | 🟢 OPEN | Batch 2027 | Listed **15 Oct 2026**. Bengaluru and PAN India. A university-programme track aimed at students pre-final year and final year. | https://www.salesforce.com/company/careers/ |
| **Cisco — Code with Cisco FY27 (Software Engineer)** | 🟢 OPEN | Batch 2027 | Campus hiring for **2027 batch graduates**. Bengaluru and PAN India. | https://jobs.cisco.com/ |
| **Rolling ATS — Accenture, Capgemini, LTIMindtree, HCLTech, Tech Mahindra, IBM** | 🟢 OPEN (no national date) | Any batch | These seven take applications **continuously** through their own portals. “Is the drive open?” is the wrong question — the portal is either accepting resumes or it is not. Apply on your own schedule, repeat every few weeks. | https://indiacampus.accenture.com/ · capgemini.com/careers · ltimindtree.com/careers · hcltech.com/careers · careers.techemahindra.com · ibm.com/careers |
| **Wipro Elite NTH 2027** | 🔴 NOT OPEN | Batch 2027 | The official Elite page was checked **19 Oct 2026** and carries no National Talent Hunt section, no dates and no registration link. Wipro's CHRO said in April 2026 there is **no fresher-hiring target for FY27** and that hiring is “completely on demand”. If you see “Wipro NTH 2027 open” content now, treat it as premature. | https://careers.wipro.com/elite |
| **GATE 2027** | 🔴 CLOSED | Final-year | Registration ran 27 Aug – 27 Sep 2026 with a late-fee window to 5 Oct 2026 — **both have passed**. Relevant only for PSU / M.Tech routes, not for the private IT campus cycle. | https://apply.gate.ac.in/ |

### What each of these means for you, in order of priority

| Priority | Drive | Why it is ranked there |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | **TCS iON NQT — November cycle** | Registration closes **12 Nov 2026** and the test is **24 Nov 2026**. This is the single highest-value thing you can do this month: NQT is the gate to TCS's BPS, Digital and Prime hiring, and it is open to pass-outs from 2022 to 2028. Pay the ₹999 and sit the test. |
| **2** | **Infosys Systems Engineer (SE)** | The campus form is live and confirmed reachable. SP / DSE in-person coding tests are scheduled for 25 Oct, 1 Nov, 12 Nov and 6 Dec 2026 — **if your college runs one of those slots, you are already inside the window.** Get your college's drive link from the placement cell. |
| **3** | **Cognizant GenC family** | Live on Superset with no published deadline, and Cognizant is targeting ~24–25k graduates in calendar 2026. Apply early, then re-apply each time a new Superset listing appears. |
| **4** | **Rolling ATS companies (Accenture, Capgemini, LTIMindtree, HCLTech, Tech Mahindra, IBM)** | No season, no deadline, no announcement. A resume that is live on six portals simultaneously is worth more than one that is live on none. Re-submit every two to three weeks. |
| **5** | **TCS BSc Ignite, TCS HackQuest, Salesforce Futureforce, Cisco Code with Cisco** | Smaller volumes than the top four, but they are genuine 2027-batch openings and they are open now. HackQuest in particular costs only a weekend and puts a ₹5-lakh cyber contest on the resume. |
| **6** | **Wipro Elite NTH** | ❌ **Skip it for now** — the official page carries no NTH section as of 19 Oct 2026, and Wipro has publicly said it has no FY27 fresher-hiring target. Check `careers.wipro.com/elite` monthly; that is the only page that matters. |

> ⚠️ **Three honest caveats.** (1) Most of these status rows are aggregator-reported — the **official portal is always authoritative**, and the link in the table is the portal. (2) **Never pay a fee** except the official TCS NQT fee; every major IT employer states it charges nothing at any stage, and Wipro has publicly confirmed it charges no fee either. (3) A **2027 batch** student is eligible for all of these; a **2028 batch** student is eligible for almost none of them, because the hiring is batch-locked to the current graduating cohort. If you are reading this and you are not a 2027 batch, jump to the [2028-batch brief](../All_2028_Batch_Openings_India_BTechCS_2026-10-25/) instead.

## 📋 Eligible Batches & Fit

> **Honest fit assessment.** A 2027 batch is the easiest batch to place in 2026 — the campus cycle is *built* for you. But the same cycle is closed to every other batch, so the fit column below is unusually sharp this year: it is not “2027 is good”, it is **“2027 is the only batch these drives will take”**.

| Batch (Graduation Year) | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **2024** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed — fails every “currently pursuing / pursuing B.Tech” clause |
| **2025** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2026** | ⚠️ Mostly ❌ | Already graduated by the Oct 2026 start date. Only the handful of postings that explicitly name a **2026 pass-out batch** (54 of them in this brief) would take them |
| **2027** | ✅ **Primary target — and the best batch year to be in right now** | This brief is built for this batch. The 2026–27 campus cycle (TCS NQT Nov 2026, Infosys SE, Cognizant GenC, Salesforce Futureforce, Cisco) is **open today**, and every one of those drives is batch-locked to the current graduating cohort |
| **2028** | ❌ Not eligible | That cycle has not opened. It opens around **Aug 2027** — see the [2028-batch brief](../All_2028_Batch_Openings_India_BTechCS_2026-10-25/) |
| **2029** | ❌ Not eligible | 1st year — not eligible for graduate-traineer or campus-fresher roles |

**Where a 2027-batch student actually wins in this brief:**

| Opportunity type | Why it fits a 2027-batch student | Count |
|---|---|---|
| **Explicit 2027 / campus-hire postings** | The posting itself names the 2027 batch — e.g. Qualcomm's *“2027 Campus Hire”* family and ION's *“2027 Leadership Development Program”* | 9 |
| **Campus mass-recruit drives** | Built for the current graduating cohort — see Section 0 | 13 drives |
| **Entry-level / fresher / graduate-trainee FTE** | No batch gate, and the strongest signal that a campus hire is happening | 117 |
| **Open entry-level FTE (no batch gate)** | Continuously-hired entry roles | 870 |
| **Internships / final-year internships** | A 2027 student is in their final year, so a **final-year internship with a PPO** is the single highest-leverage internship for them | 144 |
| **Hackathons** | Grade on the project, not the year | 67 events |

> ⚠️ **The catch that a 2027 student must not ignore.** Because the 2027 cycle is open *now*, there is a real risk of **applying to 40 things in three weeks and preparing for none of them**. TCS NQT, Infosys SE and Cognizant GenC all test **aptitude plus coding** in the same format. One solid DSA-and-aptitude grind from this week covers all three. Spread across the top four drives, not scattered across fifty listings.

---

## 📋 Eligible Branches / Disciplines

> **Honest fit assessment.** The Section 0 mass-recruit drives are the broadest part of this brief — TCS, Infosys, Cognizant, Accenture, Capgemini, HCLTech and IBM all take **B.Tech / B.E. / B.Sc / MCA / MBA** across CS, IT, ECE, EE, ME, CE and even some non-engineering groups. The tiered LinkedIn sections below are narrower, because they are CS/IT roles by construction.

| Branch / Discipline | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **Computer Science (CS / CSE)** | ✅ **Strongest** | The target audience of every tiered posting in this brief |
| **Information Technology (IT)** | ✅ Strong | Indistinguishable from CS for software, full-stack and DevOps roles; accepted in the standard “B.Tech / BCA / MCA” eligibility lines |
| **Artificial Intelligence (AI)** | ✅ Strong | The AI/ML, LLM and agentic-AI roles are pitched squarely at AI students |
| **Machine Learning (ML)** | ✅ Strong | ML internships, data-science and research-adjacent roles |
| **Data Science (DS)** | ✅ Strong | Dedicated data-analyst, data-science and data-engineering roles |
| **Electronics & Communication (ECE)** | ✅ **Stronger than usual this year** | The explicit 2027 campus-hire tier is hardware-heavy — Qualcomm's *2027 Campus Hire* family includes HW and System Engineer tracks, which are open to ECE. Section 0 drives take ECE comfortably. |
| **Electrical Engineering (EE)** | ⚠️ Possible | Embedded, VLSI, hardware and power-electronics tracks only |
| **Mathematics (Math)** | ⚠️ Possible | Actuarial and quant roles — e.g. WTW's *2027 Actuarial Analyst Graduate Program* in this brief |
| **Mechanical / Civil (ME / CE)** | ⚠️ Possible | Only in the Section 0 mass-recruit drives, which are engineering-discipline agnostic |

> 📌 For the **9 explicit 2027 openings** in Tier 1, each row carries a **“Branch reality”** line naming the track it actually sits in — software, hardware or actuarial — so a CS student does not waste an application on a hardware ASIC role and an ECE student does not miss one.

> 🔎 **54 postings were deliberately excluded** from the tiers below because the posting asks for **2 or more years of experience** (for example Cisco's *“Software Engineer (Embedded C) — 4-10 years”*). They are not 2027-batch opportunities and listing them would only dilute the list. They remain counted in the snapshot for honesty.

---

## 🧭 How To Read This Brief

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ **2027** | The posting itself names the 2027 batch or the 2027 campus-hire cycle |
| ✅ **Entry level** | Fresher / entry-level / graduate-engineer-trainee / no-experience condition |
| ✅ **Final year / PPO** | Final-year internship with an offer pathway |
| 🟢 **Open** | No batch gate and no experience gate — open to a 2027 student |
| ⚠️ **Pre-final** | Mentions pre-final year / 3rd year — a 2027 student is **past** that stage, so read carefully |
| ❌ **2026 / experienced** | Wants a 2026 pass-out or 2+ years of experience |
| 🔥 **Actively Hiring** | LinkedIn shows the “Actively Hiring” tag |
| ✅ (repo) | This company already has its own entry in this repository |

**Classification used throughout:**

| Class | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **FTE (Entry Level)** | Full-time role at entry / fresher / junior / associate level |
| **FTE — Graduate Engineer Trainee** | Full-time graduate-trainee designation |
| **Internship (no PPO stated)** | Internship with no offer pathway mentioned in the posting |
| **PPO Internship / PPI Internship** | Internship whose posting states an offer or conversion pathway |

## 🎯 Tier 3 — Open Entry-Level FTE (No Batch Gate)

> Full-time roles with no batch restriction and no hard experience bar. These are hired **continuously** rather than once a year — which is why several of them (Kulfi, TalentRabbit, NEXTHIRE, Zetheta, GreyOrange, PFI Innovation Centre) appeared on LinkedIn while the campus drives were running.

## 🎯 Tier 4 — Internships (and Final-Year Internships)

> For a 2027 batch the internship that matters is the **final-year internship**, because that is where the **PPO** is decided and where the campus offer is negotiated. The titles below that read *“Graduate Engineer Trainee”* or *“Trainee”* are the ones to look at first — at companies like GE HealthCare, Coperion, Valeo, Ingersoll Rand, Eaton, Siemens, Konecranes, Alten India, Wipro and Robro Systems, the trainee track *is* the campus pipeline.

> **Read this section carefully.** **14 of these 20 postings state a PPO**, and the stipends are genuinely competitive — **₹25,000/month (CleverTap, CitiusTech), ₹22,000/month (Intel India), ₹21,000/month (DuraLine, Shell India), ₹20,000/month (Target India, Pharmarack)**. But the recurring title is *Data Processing Intern*, which means data cleaning, spreadsheets and light scripting rather than software engineering. **For a 2027 batch the deadline is the problem, not the fit** — these are 2–6 month internships, so many of them run past the point where you need to be earning. Treat them as a **fallback with a named company on the resume**, and prioritise the PPO-linked final-year internships in Tier 4.

> **67 hackathons** are open right now with technical tracks and student eligibility, of which **28 are fully online**. For a 2027 batch a hackathon is no longer a portfolio builder for later — it is a **team project that can become the final-year project**, and several campus drives now ask for “personal or academic projects” in the eligibility line. ### The three worth a weekend, for a 2027 batch

| Hackathon | Why |
|---|---|
| **TCS HackQuest Season 11** | It is the only hackathon in this list that is **explicitly for the 2027 batch**, it is a cybersecurity contest with a **₹5 lakh** prize pool, and it is run by TCS — the same employer whose NQT cycle is open right now. Same company, same year, one weekend. |
| **NASA Space Apps Challenge 2026** | The most recognised hackathon brand on this list, with a global prize pool and a NASA-affiliated judging panel. Hybrid, so an IIITM team can travel. |
| **CodeQuest: National Tech Hackathon 2026** | National-level and fully online, hosted by DotRepo — a clean fit for building a GitHub presence before the placement interviews start. |

> ⚠️ **Always read the rules page.** Several of these ban AI assistance in the solution, several require a **registered team of 2–4** with a **college ID**, and a few are **invite-only** campus rounds where IIITM's own team quota applies. Check `regRequirementsText` on the listing before counting on any of them.

## 🧭 The 2027-Batch Application Playbook

> A 2027 batch has roughly **seven months** from this week to graduation. That is enough time to be placed — but only if the effort is sequenced. The failure mode is not “applied to too few”; it is **“applied to fifty listings and sat none of the aptitude tests”**.

```
  2027-BATCH (2023-2027) - 4th year / 7th semester as of Oct 2026
  ===========================================================================

  OCT 2026  (NOW - highest urgency of any batch in this repo)
    |-- Register: TCS iON NQT (Nov cycle)        <- reg closes 12 Nov, test 24 Nov
    |-- Register: Infosys SE campus form          <- SP/DSE tests 25 Oct/1 Nov/12 Nov/6 Dec
    |-- Apply: Cognizant GenC on Superset        <- no deadline, apply early
    |-- Apply: Salesforce Futureforce + Cisco Code with Cisco
    |-- Apply: rolling ATS (Accenture/Capgemini/LTIMindtree/HCLTech/TechM/IBM)
    |-- Sign up: TCS HackQuest Season 11         <- 1 weekend, 2027-batch only
    `-- Start the ONE prep: aptitude + DSA, covers NQT / Infosys SE / GenC

  NOV 2026 - JAN 2027
    |-- Sit the tests (TCS NQT 24 Nov, Infosys SP/DSE, GenC assessments)
    |-- Re-apply the rolling ATS portals every 2-3 weeks
    |-- Final-year internship with a PPO (Tier 4 / Tier 5)
    `-- Build 1-2 real projects for the interview round

  FEB - APR 2027
    |-- Interviews + offers + PPOs
    |-- Fallback: off-campus / niche product companies via Tier 2 + Tier 3
    `-- Keep the resume live on 6+ portals

  MAY - JUN 2027
    `-- Join, or start the next cycle with a stronger profile
  ```

### The four things that decide a 2027-batch placement

| Lever | Why it matters for a 2027 batch specifically |
|---|---|
| **One aptitude + DSA prep, reused three times** | TCS NQT, Infosys SE and Cognizant GenC test the same core skills in the same format. Preparing separately for each is the single most common waste of a final-year student's time. |
| **Apply on day one of a listing** | Fresh postings get 3–5× the applicant volume in week one. The Cognizant 2025 cycle ran registration 30 Sep – 8 Oct and the three assessments across 14–22 Oct — that is the shape of the window to hit. |
| **A final-year project that is actually yours** | Tier 2 and 3 postings ask for “personal or academic projects”. One real, deployed, documented project beats a Kaggle notebook with zero stars — and it doubles as the Tier 7 hackathon entry. |
| **Six live portals, not one** | The rolling ATS companies do not run seasons. A resume that is live on six of them at all times converts at a rate that one live-on-a-single-portal resume does not. |

### Four traps in this brief, stated plainly

1. **A PPO in the posting text is not a PPO guarantee.** The Tier 5 PPO flags are extracted from the listing body — phrases like *“high-performing interns are evaluated for conversion”*. That is a statement of intent. Read the actual conversion criteria.
2. **A 2027 batch can apply to the 2027 cycle, but many listings will still filter on it.** Where a posting names no batch at all, apply anyway — but do not assume you are the only candidate pool. The Tier 2 and Tier 3 openings are deliberately batch-agnostic.
3. **“Fresher” on a LinkedIn posting does not always mean campus hiring.** Some of the Tier 3 openings are small-company roles that will train you but will not move your package. Section 0 is where the package moves.
4. **Wipro is the trap of the week.** A lot of content online says “Wipro Elite NTH 2027 registration open”. The official page says otherwise as of 19 Oct 2026, and Wipro's own CHRO has said there is no FY27 fresher target. **Check `careers.wipro.com/elite` yourself and trust that page over every WhatsApp forward.**

## ⚠️ Caveats & Method

### What was done

```
  1. LinkedIn guest job-search API (public, no login)
     - 12 explicit batch keywords + 10 final-year/PPO/PPI keywords
       + 15 fresher/GET entry-level keywords
       + 70 high-volume 2027 hirers (TCS, Infosys, Cognizant, Wipro, Accenture,
         Amdocs, LTIMindtree, Capgemini, Deloitte, HCLTech, Virtusa, Tech Mahindra,
         DXC, EPAM, Zoho, Freshworks, Value Labs, Tekion, PhonePe, Flipkart, Swiggy,
         Zomato, Meesho, Dream11, Games24x7, Razorpay, CRED, Adobe, Google, Microsoft,
         Amazon, PayPal, Goldman Sachs, JPMorgan, Morgan Stanley, ServiceNow,
         Salesforce, Qualcomm, NVIDIA, AMD, Intel, Samsung, Apple, Cisco, Oracle, SAP,
         IBM, Honeywell, Siemens, Bosch, Harman, Continental, MediaTek, OnePlus,
         Nothing, Slice, Jupiter, Pine Labs, and banks)
       + 18 city hubs x 3 keywords
     - filters: f_JT=I (internship), f_E=1,2,3 (entry level)
     -> 232 requests, 3,384 unique job IDs, 2,040 full JDs fetched

  2. Every fetched JD parsed for: explicit batch year (incl. '2027 Campus Hire'),
     final-year / PPO / PPI language, fresher / GET / no-experience language,
     minimum years of experience, stipend, duration, work model, seniority level.

  3. Filters applied: India-only (location AND title), CS/IT title, no senior,
     no PhD-only, no diploma / data-entry / management-trainee roles,
     no sales/HR/finance/design roles, deduped against this repo's existing entries.

  4. Section 0: 13 mass-recruit drives status-checked on 25 Oct 2026 against official
     portals (TCS iON, TCS BPS, TCS HackQuest, Infosys campus form, Cognizant
     careers, Accenture India campus, Wipro Elite, Capgemini, HCLTech, LTIMindtree,
     Tech Mahindra, IBM, Salesforce, Cisco) plus aggregator trackers, with the
     source named on every row.

  5. Aggregators: myinternships.in JSON-LD (2027-batch page, 20 listings fetched
     individually) + Unstop public API (189 open hackathons, filtered to 67
     student-eligible technical events).
```

### What this brief is not

| Limitation | Detail |
|---|---|
| **LinkedIn's guest API caps results** | It returns roughly the top 25–50 per query. Mass-recruit campus roles are under-represented because the API de-duplicates by pagination — and the biggest 2027 opportunities (Section 0) are not on LinkedIn at all. |
| **Section 0 status is a snapshot, not a guarantee** | Every row was checked on **25 Oct 2026**. A drive can open or close without announcement — Cognizant's 2025 cycle ran registration 30 Sep – 8 Oct and assessments 14–22 Oct, a window that is easy to miss. **Re-check the official portal before you rely on any date.** |
| **Aggregator status rows can be stale** | Some Section 0 rows come from trackers rather than the company portal. Where the two disagree (Infosys SP/DSE batch eligibility), the row says so explicitly instead of picking one. |
| **“Open” does not mean “free of hidden gates”** | A posting that names no batch may still filter on it internally. Apply anyway, but read the full JD. |
| **Stipends are as posted** | No negotiation, no verification with the company. Where a number was absent, the row says *Not disclosed* rather than guessing. |
| **PPO flags are text matches** | A PPO flag means the posting *mentions* an offer or conversion pathway. It does not mean a guaranteed offer. |
| **This is a CS/IT brief** | Section 0 is the exception — the mass-recruit drives take most engineering branches, and that is flagged separately in the branch table. |
